## 2026-11-17 Update-request URL Log Redaction

- **Baseline and scope:** continued from dirty `main` at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. Existing worktree edits, `.Jules/palette.md`, user-provided visual data, and inaccessible `.tmp_pytest/` and `test-run-temp/` paths were preserved. The MirrorChyan request constructs a URL containing the decrypted CDK; `requests` network exceptions can include the request URL. Raw exception text was written to logs by update-check fallback, `get_assets_url()`, and the package download handler. This was verified from source, not from a live request or incident log.
- **Change:** those four network/update exception log sites now record `type(error).__name__` rather than exception text. MirrorChyan-to-GitHub fallback, failure signals, and user-facing messages are unchanged. No request URL, query parameter, or credential value is added to the new logs.

## 2026-11-17 Shop EGO Power-up Failure Propagation

- **Baseline and scope:** continued from dirty worktree `main` at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. Existing user edits, screenshots, ignored local notes and inaccessible temp paths were preserved. The source finding is from control-flow inspection; no recent log or live incident was used as evidence.
- **Finding and fix:** `Shop.ego_gift_to_power_up()` stops after its existing 31-attempt bound but previously fell through with implicit `None`. Its callers distinguish failure with `is False`, so `None` could enter the success branch and append an unconfirmed gift to `enhance_gifts_list`. The exhausted path now returns `False`. No retry, click, gift ranking, or UI behavior changed.
- **Regression:** `test_ego_power_up_reports_failure_after_bounded_attempts` mocks capture, input, and recognition, asserts failure, and verifies exactly 31 screenshot waits. It failed on the old source with `assert None is False`; after the fix, `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py` passed **33 tests**.
- **Source validation identity/results:** snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_powerup_return_green_20261117_01\source`; source SHA-256 `tasks/mirror/in_shop.py` `25DF70688A54306DA800B9B10FAD4F2D3E039E7730A6ACDE4C534A1AB2AA91F2`, test SHA-256 `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py` `769DA326858E4B933565EB7DC9EFD46A70C4DAE4D853A43BB666196C6385F2A0`; both matched the worktree. Focused tests passed **33**. Full command was `python -m pytest -ra -p no:cacheprovider --tb=short --basetemp <unique-temp>` and passed **534 tests, 2 skipped of 536 collected in 73.47s**. Skips were the manifest-path and updater-boundary tests requiring unavailable Windows symlink creation. Project Ruff (`ruff check --no-cache app module tasks utils scripts tests main.py main_dev.py updater.py`) and `compileall -q app module tasks utils scripts main.py main_dev.py updater.py` passed. The source snapshot began without root config; generated/local config content was not read.
- **Package build and artifact identity:** separate disposable build source `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_build_powerup_return_20261117_01\source`, version `validation-powerup-return-20261117`, Python 3.12.13 / PyInstaller 6.16.0. The first attempt built both PyInstaller targets but stopped when `pyside6-lrelease` was absent from `PATH`; the retry added the existing environment scripts and a build-copy-local `7z.exe` alias for bundled `assets/binary/7za.exe`. Both targets then built and generated 538 translations. The script warned that optional PP-OCRv5 detector/recognizer files were absent. Root `config.yaml` appeared in the disposable build source during the initial build attempt; neither it nor the worktree config was read. Independent extraction found no root config in the package; the only `config.yaml` was `_internal/rapidocr/config.yaml`. The archive also contained no tests, fixtures, `.pyc`, `__pycache__`, or `.tmp` paths.
- **Archive verification:** `AALC_validation-powerup-return-20261117.7z`, 139,912,679 bytes, SHA-256 `DC67959CE2210D3BEDC73F76423F048B332FDCCD694EE8B58715DCC39BF10744`. `7za t` and separate extraction passed: 204 folders, 1,542 files, 354,653,589 unpacked bytes. Source and extracted runtime each had 688 asset paths, no missing/extra paths, and only `assets/config/version.txt` differed because the build injects the requested version. `AALC.exe` is 12,301,375 bytes, SHA-256 `B1B573E554FB2DB08FFF2296278BE1E1679CF6B2DFD8F409E72A176075962380`; updater is 8,753,406 bytes, SHA-256 `A6746C318FEF676D291CB856A01ED3D4DC12F925965AC821404D6FE65E64121D`.
- **Runtime boundary and next steps:** neither executable was launched. The user's “已关闭” was treated as app-state context, not authorization for either new hash. Do not launch app/updater/game; keep package runtime, real shop behavior, install identity, and deployment unqualified unless separately authorized for the exact binary hash and isolated runtime. Continue only with offline work and preserve the dirty worktree. `.tmp_pytest/` and `test-run-temp/` remain inaccessible and untouched. The available goal state exposes no remaining five-hour quota percentage (`remainingTokens` is unavailable), so the 10% quota threshold cannot be verified in this environment.

## 2026-11-17 Observe EGO Detail Capture Guard

- **Baseline and evidence:** continued from committed `main` at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`; the worktree was already dirty and all unrelated changes were preserved. Root `config.yaml` was not read. The newest log files were dated 2026-08-20, so no current incident log was available; filenames/metadata only were checked and log contents were not read.
- **Finding and change:** after clicking a selected Observe EGO gift, `select_observe_ego_gift()` called `auto.take_screenshot()` without checking for `None`, then OCRed the details-panel button. `Automation.take_screenshot()` retains the prior successful `self.screenshot` when a capture fails, and `_run_ocr_for_text()` reads that stored frame. This could make the detail-panel OCR act on a stale inventory frame. The call now uses the existing bounded `wait_for_screenshot()` helper and propagates capture failure before detail OCR or another selection click. Gift coordinates, ranking, configuration semantics, and game input were not changed.
- **Regression coverage:** a new offline test verifies capture timeout propagates after the grid click and before details OCR; the existing three-item-limit test and two `test_milestone4.py` Observe EGO tests now mock the shared waiter so they cannot touch the native screenshot backend. The new test failed against the previous source with `Failed: DID NOT RAISE RuntimeError`.
- **Validation identity:** final config-free source snapshot: `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_observe_details_20261117_05\source`. `tasks/mirror/mirror.py` SHA-256 `0D633844107EBBF1675A89A5CE90C63317CE5F0542D77CAC7E5A123322F3CC7F`; `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py` SHA-256 `9FE169AC1016D33580C31C20B1CDF939E021F7AF503297C6EA69C49F16A19019`; `tests/unit/module/automation/test_milestone4.py` SHA-256 `30524203ABF0A487B40DA3B5249811F438417DB780BFF848E538E2AD925F8CB5`. All three hashes matched the worktree before validation. The snapshot began without root `config.yaml`; pytest may generate a copy-local config, which was not read.
- **Test and static results:** four focused regressions passed in **15.49s**. `python -m pytest -ra -p no:cacheprovider --tb=short --basetemp C:\Users\AscAed\AppData\Local\Temp\aalc-observe-details-full-20261117-02` passed **532 tests, 2 skipped of 534 collected in 73.28s**. The skips require Windows symlink creation. `ruff check --no-cache app module tasks utils scripts tests main.py main_dev.py updater.py` reported **All checks passed**; `python -m compileall -q app module tasks utils scripts main.py main_dev.py updater.py` passed. An intermediate run exposed three tests that needed the shared waiter mocked; they were corrected, and the final full-suite run passed.
- **Package build and identity:** a separate package source at `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_build_observe_details_20261117_01\source` was copied without `.git`, `.venv`, tests/fixtures, logs, local configuration, or existing `dist/`; its `mirror.py` hash matched the worktree. `python scripts\build.py --version validation-observe-details-20261117` built both PyInstaller targets and generated 538 translations. The archive `AALC_validation-observe-details-20261117.7z` SHA-256 is `B166CCBB1973607FBB56A3854D038D4666F392C471B9840AB8262134B92A2D29`. Independent 7-Zip testing and extraction passed for 204 folders and 1,542 files. Source/package asset comparison passed for all 688 assets, with no missing or extra paths and only the injected `assets/config/version.txt` hash difference; no test or screenshot paths appeared in the archive. App SHA-256: `68EDCAD6BC47ED084E0D79F83631364CF2E578059FCA09CD250EE3F98ADAA7D9`; updater SHA-256: `6E186673EA8BAE6AD816C72E164A87ECA04651D93F06FD1E8D9441A8F348F676`. Optional PP-OCRv5 detector/recognizer models were absent, as in prior builds.
- **Remaining qualification:** neither new executable was launched; this patch has source/test and archive evidence only. The test change does not validate the real Observe EGO detail panel, OCR result, selection coordinates, or gameplay transition. There is no representative detail-panel fixture, and the nine screenshot fixtures remain uncommitted while their distribution basis is unresolved. Any app or updater runtime check needs fresh action-time confirmation naming its exact new hash and an isolated runtime.
- **Next steps:** keep visual selection, transitions, and live-game behavior unqualified until an authorized detail-panel sample or separately authorized runtime check is available. For packaged updater qualification, use the new updater hash above only after explicit confirmation and a fresh isolated-directory/process preflight. Keep installed identity and deployment gates open.

## 2026-11-17 Updater Relative-Path Follow-up (Package Superseded by Observe EGO Build)

- **Baseline and preservation:** continued from `main` at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty. Preserved the existing updater no-payload test and all unrelated changes; `.tmp_pytest/` and `test-run-temp/` remain inaccessible and untouched. The root `config.yaml` exists but was not read.
- **Reproduction and fix:** `check_temp_dir_and_run()` compared raw relative `sys.argv[0]` against an absolute `update_temp` destination. With `.\update_temp\AALC Updater.exe` as argv[0], the old code entered its copy/relaunch branch and exited with status 0 instead of running the already-staged updater. That branch also removes `.\Update.exe` before copying. The source now normalizes argv[0] with `abspath` and compares normalized paths with `normcase`. A regression asserts it does not copy/relaunch and preserves an `Update.exe` sentinel. The regression failed against the old source and passed after the fix.
- **Validation identity:** the latest config-free validation snapshot is `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_updater_relative_full_20261117_02\source`, created from the previous full snapshot while excluding `.git`, `config.yaml*`, `config_backup`, `.venv`, logs, build output, runtime directories, and common caches; it started without root `config.yaml` or `dist/`. Worktree/snapshot hashes match: `updater.py` `905ECA0E0EB1598C74552560C2E4790408399622B55540BC02F8375240153503`; `tests/unit/updater/test_updater_bounds.py` `DA30866E2A03FAA8CDCF279F126A1CF7D6BB5EF3820089F28039EAE0D0802C0E`. The test includes both a normal relative path and a mixed-case, forward-slash path to the same staged file. A separate package source at `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_build_updater_relative_20261117_01\source` contains the same production updater hash; the post-build test change only expands coverage and does not change the executable source.
- **Results:** from that updater-relative validation snapshot, the updater file passed **50 tests, 1 skipped**; the full suite passed **531 tests, 2 skipped of 533 collected**. All skips require Windows symlink creation. Full project Ruff reported **All checks passed** and `compileall -q app module tasks utils scripts main.py main_dev.py updater.py` passed. `git diff --check` is recorded after the documentation sync below. The package was built with `scripts/build.py --version validation-2026-11-17-updater-relative`; both PyInstaller targets completed and generated 538 translations, with warnings that optional PP-OCRv5 detector and recognizer models were absent. The 7-Zip package archive SHA-256 is `963B796511067EDC0B2416AB08B4E587011BAA264AC3CF7D70972C491177401C`; independent 7-Zip test and extraction passed for 204 folders and 1,542 files. Source and extracted package each contain 688 assets, with no missing/extra files or unexpected hash differences beyond the injected `assets/config/version.txt`. The archive contains no test directory. `AALC.exe` SHA-256 is `20720E711E5F73B2E4C1CA1D703856A1B099ECBBA2EDABE93AA489E8F31F983E`; `AALC Updater.exe` SHA-256 is `06BC505917A1033A14F0ED239D18D2294986768BF97A60707E3D918F668300C1`.
- **Remaining qualification:** both new executables passed build/archive checks but remain unlaunched. The earlier updater `0DED3F351DE1877F2594827AB8F5654C84E073AAC850263A54CA141709BF969B` predates the fix and is superseded by the hash above. No real process was terminated, no update payload was applied, and no network request was made. A no-payload run from the packaged install may invoke the bundled 7-Zip process against a missing payload before returning failure; keep it in an isolated writable runtime. The user's "已关闭" was treated only as an app-state report, not action-time approval for a new artifact.
- **Documentation sync at this checkpoint:** the docs then pointed to the 531/533 source result and the `06BC...300C1` packaged updater; those results and artifact identities have since been superseded by the Observe EGO checkpoint above. Older 529/531 and `0DED...` references remain historical.
- **Next steps at this checkpoint:** (1) Any packaged no-payload CLI qualification had to wait for fresh confirmation naming updater hash `06BC505917A1033A14F0ED239D18D2294986768BF97A60707E3D918F668300C1`; use an isolated writable runtime and do not apply an update or launch the game. The latest updater hash is recorded in the Observe EGO checkpoint above. (2) Keep current-package UI capture/clean exit, installed identity, and deployment gates open. (3) Keep visual fixture rights/provenance and live-game recognition/interaction qualification open.

## Recovered Scope and Sources

- The referenced Codex conversations were read: `01a1d073-0cd4-7341-8ba2-be4272a4b17a` (AALC project plan) and `01a1cf2e-8958-7af1-99fe-aec47024af74` (AALC project documents). The current request authorizes implementation and verification; it supersedes earlier documentation-only scope.
- Project facts and stage priorities are in [PROJECT.md](../PROJECT.md) and the [implementation roadmap](../development/implementation-roadmap.md). Earlier evidence and decisions are retained in the [stage and gap assessment](2026-11-13-stage-gap-assessment.md), [implementation checkpoint](2026-11-13-implementation-checkpoint.md), and [Mirror fixture inventory](2026-11-13-mirror-fixture-inventory.md).
- The project is a Windows/Python 3.12 maintenance fork focused on reliability and diagnostics. It is not general game AI, proof of optimal play, or evidence of a deployed artifact.
- Codex quota usage is not exposed in the available runtime. Work was paused at an independently verified documentation/implementation boundary; no quota threshold is claimed.
- A source trace verified `Automation.take_screenshot()` checks the stop callback and retries backend `None` results internally. Background mode raises after three failed captures; foreground mode allows a 60-second window, performs one game restart, then raises if capture still fails in the next window. Therefore, the task-level `if auto.take_screenshot() is None` branches are defensive against an API return not produced by the current implementation; they do not establish unbounded normal capture-failure loops. An in-flight native capture can still exceed those limits.
- Other direct empty-capture checks are also bounded or single-shot: event handling and initial Mirror route strategies fail closed after one capture; battle mode detection retries twice; Mirror observe selection and the shop grid scan are limited to 12, 5, and 4 iterations; retry/re-entry paths check the existing 90-second freeze deadline. `get_current_enkephalin()` has a five-iteration OCR/capture loop, and screenshot exceptions now propagate outside its OCR retry handler.
- The Enkephalin panel wait checks the worker stop callback each iteration; after its 60-second warning it emits `mediator.link_start`, which the farming UI connects to its stop shortcut. The empty-frame caller branch is not the active cancellation mechanism.
- Continuation audit on 2026-11-14 reread both referenced Codex threads. The earlier project thread's paused review and the later documentation plan were treated as historical context; current checkout and worktree evidence remain authoritative.

Observe EGO runtime parsing rejects malformed systems and out-of-range values and caps valid configured selections at three. `get_reward_card()` now caps all capture/action iterations at 31 and raises on exhaustion; regressions cover a stuck continue-button click and success on the last allowed iteration. The current shop audit also bounds purchase, sale-entry, ordinary-fusion, and system-fusion loops at 31 plus configured refresh budgets, 64, 31, and 31 iterations respectively; four offline reproductions failed before these guards and passed afterward. `Mirror.run()` also bounds repeated floor-exit, missing-control, EGO confirmation, Infinity popup, and road-map no-marker paths. ## Completed Batch

- Added [tasks/base/screenshot.py](../../tasks/base/screenshot.py), a shared `wait_for_screenshot()` helper with a default 300-attempt limit and an injectable automation object for deterministic tests.
- Updated selected daily workflows in `tasks/daily/get_prize.py` (pass/mail claims) and `tasks/daily/luxcavation.py` (EXP/thread entry) so persistent `None` captures fail rather than spin indefinitely.
- Updated `Battle.to_battle()` to stop after 16 failed captures, and selected `Battle.fight()` capture paths to use the shared bounded waiter. Mirror death-state polling now returns to the existing outer finite-battle timeout instead of bypassing it through an inner unbounded loop.
- Kept the Mirror shop wrapper as a local test seam while delegating to the shared helper in `tasks/mirror/in_shop.py`.
- Updated `BattleWorker` to log unexpected runtime failures and emit its existing user-facing error signal.
- Added deterministic failure-boundary coverage in `tests/unit/tasks/base/test_screenshot.py`, `tests/unit/tasks/daily/test_screenshot_failure.py`, `tests/unit/tasks/battle/test_battle.py`, `tests/unit/app/test_task_stop_lifecycle.py`, and `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py`.
- No live game, native GUI, simulator, remote service, or installed package was exercised.
- In this continuation, added parameterized coverage in `tests/unit/module/automation/test_state_flow.py` for background screenshot backends returning empty frames or raising errors. Both stop after three attempts; no production code changed in this continuation.
- Updater mutation-point review found one distinct staged-copy path not covered by the prior `check_temp_dir_and_run()` recovery test: `_handoff_to_new_updater()` copies the extracted updater with `shutil.copy2`. Added `test_handoff_recovers_after_process_exit_during_updater_copy`; it terminates only its controlled Python child after writing a partial file under pytest's temporary install tree, then verifies a fresh handoff overwrites the fragment and launches the complete staged file. No updater production code changed.
- `Updater.terminate_processes()` previously attempted substring matching on every `proc.info["name"]`; psutil 7.1.0 uses `None` by default when prefetching an attribute raises `AccessDenied` or `ZombieProcess` (version is pinned in `uv.lock`). A nameless process could therefore raise `TypeError` before the intended target process was reached. Added a mocked-process regression, observed it fail with `TypeError` before the fix, then changed the filter to skip non-string names without changing matching or termination behavior. The test does not enumerate or terminate real processes.
- Full-update deletion review found two related failure cases. A `PermissionError` after partial removal of the old `assets/images` tree was logged and ignored, allowing copy, payload cleanup, and apparent success. Separately, Python 3.12 `shutil.rmtree()` can propagate `FileNotFoundError` from a child operation; the updater treated every such exception as proof that the root was absent. A regression reproduced the latter as a successful update with stale old resources still present. `cover_folder()` now treats `FileNotFoundError` as benign only if `os.path.lexists(delete_folder_path)` is false; if the root remains, it raises a contextual `RuntimeError`. Other deletion errors also abort. The tests verify failed updates retain the partial old tree and payload, write no completion marker, do not launch the application, and preserve an outside sentinel. They also verify a missing old-resource root is benign and a fresh updater instance can later reuse retained payload, remove the remaining old file, install the new image, and clean the payload and archive.
- Initial EGO gift selection previously had an inner slash-gift scroll loop that could keep dragging forever when the expected blunt-gift marker never appeared, bypassing the outer 30-attempt recognition limit. It now allows at most 12 drags and raises `cannotOperateGameError` if the scroll target remains unresolved. Regressions verify failure at exhaustion without selecting a card and successful continuation when the target appears on attempt 12. These tests verify control flow with mocks; they do not verify visual detection or scroll distance on a live layout.

## Continuation Batch: Mirror Route Entry

- Source review found `road_to_mir()` decremented its 30-count only on the fall-through path. Repeatedly recognized actions such as clicking `home/drive_assets.png` took `continue` and skipped the counter indefinitely. Each call to `retry()` creates a new local timer; `check_times()` applies a 90-second non-loading deadline and a separate 300-second loading-screen limit within that call, so elapsed time does not aggregate across the outer iterations.
- `tasks/mirror/mirror.py` now uses a 31-iteration `for` loop. Existing no-match counting, model changes, and menu recovery remain intact; loop exhaustion invokes the same `back_init_menu()` recovery. This bounds control flow between captures but does not interrupt a native capture call already in progress.
- Added `test_road_to_mirror_bounds_repeated_successful_drive_clicks` to `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py`. ## Continuation Batch: Aggressive Fusion Missing Anchor

- The shop can be configured for aggressive fusion while `the_first_line_position` is still `None`. If the aggressive grid scan cannot find `fuse_label.png`, it returns without initializing that cutoff; the caller then proceeds to ordinary fusion. When ordinary fusion detects at least one sellable gift, `_processing_coordinates()` previously compared its y-coordinate with `None` and raised `TypeError`. This failure was reproduced in a config-free snapshot with mocked shop detections.
- `Shop._processing_coordinates()` now logs a warning and returns an empty candidate list when aggressive mode has candidates but no row anchor. Ordinary fusion therefore makes no candidate gift clicks in that unresolved state. Empty candidate lists retain the existing behavior without an extra warning; non-aggressive coordinate processing is unchanged.
- Added `test_standard_fusion_skips_candidates_without_aggressive_row_anchor` in `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py`. The old source failed with `TypeError: '>=' not supported between instances of 'int' and 'NoneType'` at the row filter. ## Continuation Batch: Second-System Fusion Flag

- Source and UI tracing found a persisted type mismatch. `TeamSetting.second_system_action` is declared `List[int]`, and `ConfigModel` preserves the model field type; an enabled action normalizes to integer `1` (`TeamSetting(second_system_action=[True, False, False, False]).second_system_action[0]` is `1`, `int`, and `is True` is false). The UI's checkbox mapping writes boolean states, while the typed config schema stores the action vector as integers.
- `Shop.after_fuse_IV()` and `Shop.enter_fuse()` both compared the first action flag with `is True`. With the typed integer, selecting “合成第二体系四级饰品” therefore followed the invalid-setting sell fallback, and entering fusion selected the primary-system keyword instead of the configured second-system keyword. Both branches now use the configured flag's truth value, consistent with the other `second_system_action` checks.

## Continuation Batch: Observe EGO Selection Limit

- The GUI parser already capped Observe EGO selections at three, but `TeamSetting.observe_ego_gift_selected` is a `List[str]` without a length constraint and `Mirror.__init__` copied that list directly. The runtime therefore could process more configured entries than the UI allowed. A red regression with four valid entries observed four selection attempts where the limit requires three.
- Added shared `MAX_OBSERVE_GIFT_SELECTIONS = 3` in `module/domain_constants.py`; both the GUI parser and `Mirror.select_observe_ego_gift()` use it. Runtime skips malformed entries without consuming the count and stops before processing a fourth valid entry.

## Next Steps

1. Do not change the Enkephalin caller-level `None` branches unless a concrete supported backend is shown to return `None` from `Automation.take_screenshot()`. If that contract changes, add a reproduction at the controller/backend boundary and preserve stop propagation.
2. The updater handoff staged-copy exit window, missing process-name case, and full-update old-resource deletion failure now have offline regression coverage. No real processes were terminated. Reopen Stage 4 only for another concrete uncovered boundary or failure report; live 7-Zip termination needs explicit authorization, and replay-on-restart remains the observed recovery contract, not transactional rollback.
3. The initial EGO scroll stops after 12 unsuccessful drags; Mirror entry detection and prior-run reward collection each have a 31-iteration cap; `Mirror.run()` skips stale state dispatch after a restart result; aggressive fusion skips candidates without a row anchor; second-system fusion honors the integer action flag; and malformed or excessive Observe EGO config values are skipped before click calculation. Keep Stage 3 visual qualification open until approved sanitized full-frame fixtures include game version, resolution, scale, UI state, and expected detections. Do not infer recognition, reconnect, fusion, reward-screen, or click success from mocks.
4. Treat the 300/16 caller attempt caps as defensive attempt bounds only. If an actual stuck native capture is observed, specify a safe cancellation/recovery contract before considering process isolation or a wall-clock timeout.
5. Run any package launch/updater qualification only in a separately authorized isolated writable runtime. Startup may require elevation and initiate network checks. Do not use the user's local configuration or infer deployment from this validation archive.
6. Keep RapidOCR 3.4.1 and the current single-headless-OpenCV lock until an offline-safe model migration is implemented. The isolated investigation below found no drop-in release: 3.9.0's published wheel still declares `opencv_python`; 3.10.0 declares `opencv-python-headless` but its constructor rejects the current config (`Global.model_root_dir` is absent), and the wheel has no bundled PP-OCRv4 detector or recognizer models. Do not upgrade or install both OpenCV packages to silence `uv pip check`.
7. For every next batch: inspect current status and exact files first, write a focused regression, validate in a fresh config-free snapshot with a unique basetemp, run relevant Ruff/tests, update the roadmap and this dated handoff, then inspect `git diff --check` and the final status. Do not commit or push without explicit instruction.

## Continuation Batch: RapidOCR/OpenCV Compatibility Investigation

- Baseline: `main` at `79f2829a1953c3ea96f3a53d47959f6220a5f9c3`, Python 3.12.13, locked RapidOCR 3.4.1 and OpenCV headless 4.11.0.86. `pyproject.toml` requires `rapidocr>=3.4.1` and includes `opencv-python-headless`; `tool.uv.override-dependencies` excludes `opencv-python` with an always-false Python marker.
- Reproduced the installed-environment result with `uv pip check --offline --python .venv\Scripts\python.exe`: 85 packages checked, one incompatibility, because RapidOCR requires `opencv-python>=4.5.1.48`. No dependency was installed into the project environment.
- Downloaded the published RapidOCR 3.9.0 and 3.10.0 wheels with `--no-deps` into separate temporary audit directories outside the repository. The wheel metadata for 3.9.0 still declares `opencv_python>=4.5.1.48`; 3.10.0 declares `opencv-python-headless>=4.5.1.48`.
- With RapidOCR 3.10.0 temporarily first on `PYTHONPATH`, the project imports (`EngineType`, `LangDet`, `LangRec`, `ModelType`, `OCRVersion`, `RapidOCR`, and `RapidOCROutput`) remained available. Constructing the engine with the current `assets/config/default_rapidocr.yaml` and current `OCR.__init__` parameters failed before inference with `omegaconf.errors.ConfigAttributeError: Missing key model_root_dir` at `Global.model_root_dir`.
- The 3.10.0 wheel contains PP-OCRv6 detector/recognizer models and the classifier, but no PP-OCRv4 detector/recognizer models. Its `default_models.yaml` routes PP-OCRv4 to remote ModelScope URLs. The project explicitly selects PP-OCRv4 and its existing build collects models from the RapidOCR package, so a direct upgrade would change the config contract and risk first-use network downloads or a frozen app missing OCR models. The 3.9.0 wheel also lacks the project's PP-OCRv4 model pair.
- Decision: no code, config, dependency, or lockfile change is safe as a metadata-only fix. Keep the current locked environment; resolve this only with an explicit offline model-root/path design, bundled model assets, a representative OCR fixture, `uv pip check`, and isolated package startup validation. Upstream release notes: [RapidOCR 3.9.0](https://github.com/RapidAI/RapidOCR/releases/tag/v3.9.0) and [RapidOCR 3.10.0](https://github.com/RapidAI/RapidOCR/releases/tag/v3.10.0).

## 2026-11-14 Stop-aware Background Input Lease

- **Log evidence:** `logs/debugLog.log` records the user requesting a safe stop at 17:32:29.740 on 2026-08-19. The worker exceeded its 2,000 ms stop wait while a background-input click was waiting for user idle; it acquired a lease at 17:32:32.608 and sent mouse move/down/up events after the stop request. Lease release then failed because original foreground HWND `2167418` had become invalid. The remaining matching lines form this same stack; `debugLog.log.1` contains no matching restoration error. This does not establish whether the original HWND disappeared because of user action or application cleanup.
- **Cause:** `script_task()` and `TaskEngine` installed the worker stop callback on `auto` and the active input handler. `AbstractInput.wait_pause()` checked it, but `WindowMoveInput._wait_for_user_idle()` did not check it during its 50 ms idle polls or 50 ms settle delay. As a result, stop surfaced only after the click path had sent input.
- **Change:** `AbstractInput._raise_if_stop_requested()` now provides the existing stop semantics to pause and idle waits. Background idle polling checks each iteration, after the settle delay and before reporting timeout. Lease setup checks before capturing window state and immediately before moving the game window. A stop during idle wait raises `userStopError` before window movement, activation or `SendInput`. Foreground restoration remains fail-closed if the prior HWND has disappeared.
- **Regression evidence:** added `test_window_move_click_stop_during_idle_wait_aborts_before_input`. In a fresh archive at base `d6c481df` with only this test overlaid, it failed because `_set_window_pos(300, 400)` was called before stop surfaced. This confirms the old behavior at the isolated test boundary.
- **Focused validation:** fresh archive `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_stop_idle_green01\source` overlaid the three current worktree files and passed the input contract, input, state-flow, task-engine stop and app task-stop lifecycle groups: **96 passed**. Ruff passed on the two production files and the regression test. Overlay SHA-256 values: `module/automation/input_handlers/__init__.py` `65D2933BF8BE0B80AAD0C45504B8AE32C79AE521EB9547D53A3F1978825CBB8A`; `module/automation/input_handlers/input.py` `448CD2FAD25E33CC94BC277688BF65197DA1A2F86039747F9900B690C191EE5D`; `tests/unit/module/automation/test_input_contract.py` `0100FD37233C91FCE43F89FC247A2FEB22044BD5B69DBBF39946C4E647E7B939`.
- **Full validation:** the first sandbox attempt produced `E` markers and failed during pytest temp-directory cleanup with `PermissionError`; it is not counted. A fresh authorized config-free archive at `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_stop_idle_full02\source` with the same three SHA-256-verified overlays passed the full suite: **374 passed, 2 skipped in 46.72s**. Both skips require unavailable Windows symlink creation (`test_manifest_paths.py:238`, `test_updater_bounds.py:1656`). No package build or application/game run was performed. The earlier 7-Zip build covers base HEAD without these worktree changes.
- **Next:** retain fail-closed invalid-HWND handling unless a repeatable case and a safe focus-restoration requirement are established. Continue the independent Mirror visual gate at V1: qualify or reject tutorial-image leads by source, version, privacy and item-specific rights, then establish an authorized full-frame baseline. Keep runtime/package launch gated by an agreed writable runtime and network/configuration boundary.

## 2026-11-14 Team-list Scroll Fallback Regression

- **Source and history:** current AALC base is `main` at `d6c481dfc370f4388221c0fd3651fd05195a4221`. `logs/debugLog.log` has two team-selection warnings at 17:09:21 and 17:19:00 on 2026-08-19 reporting a missing `Automation.batch_mouse_scroll` attribute. Commit `3716d5c493ffae097dd59f497f403e3d1c4277e3` was committed later that day at 17:41:15 and added optional capability-aware binding: `Automation.init_input()` exposes the batch wrapper only when the selected input handler supports it, otherwise binds `None`. Team selection then uses repeated `mouse_scroll` calls, with a verified drag fallback if the pane does not move. The log warnings predate that implementation and do not establish a current failure.
- **Additional log disposition:** July 2026 logs also show `Invalid version: 'DEFAULT VERSION'` during update checks. Current `UpdateThread.run()` validates the local version before either network source; `test_update_check_invalid_local_version_skips_network` asserts failure and that the Mirror request is not called. This historical placeholder-version trace did not require a change in this batch.

## 2026-11-14 Theme-pack Difficulty OCR Retry

- **Source and finding:** current baseline is `main` at `d6c481dfc370f4388221c0fd3651fd05195a4221`, matching `origin/main`. Historical `logs/debugLog.log.1` entries on 2026-07-19 at 11:16:25 and 11:16:37 record `UnboundLocalError` for `difficulty` in `tasks/mirror/select_theme_pack.py`; current code already initializes it to `None`. A separate current defect remained: `Automation.find_text_element(..., only_text=True)` returns a list of OCR strings or `False` for no text, while the theme selector only accepted `str`. Its non-string branch continued before decrementing `loop_count`, so a missing-template/no-text state could repeat indefinitely.
- **Change:** the selector now scans scalar strings and list/tuple results for `normal` or `hard`. If no difficulty is found, it retains the existing legend return check and then reaches the shared counter decrement and one-second wait. This uses the existing outer retry/recovery path without changing configuration or dependencies.
- **Regression coverage:** `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_theme_pack_selection_recovery.py` checks text-list recognition reaches the normal-difficulty coordinate fallback and checks both `False` and `[]` results are bounded to five OCR attempts before menu recovery. The result uses mocked OCR, screenshots, assets and input; it does not establish real OCR accuracy.

## 2026-11-14 Bounded Shop Purchase, Sale, and Fusion Actions

- **Source finding:** a focused audit found four action paths that could repeat after a recognized click without observing a state transition: `buy_gifts()` could reselect Level III stock after every successful purchase; `sell_gifts()` could repeatedly click the sale-entry control; ordinary fusion could repeatedly click the fusion entry while a claim prompt remained; and system fusion could repeatedly select the same eligible gift after a claim click. These are control-flow findings, not confirmed live incidents. The local historical logs supplied no reproduction evidence.
- **Reproduction:** four bounded mocks were added to `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py`. Against the pre-fix implementation, each reached its test sentinel without returning or failing through the task recovery path. The corrected focused file passes 31 tests; the complete Mirror unit group passes 82 tests.
- **Implementation:** purchase scanning is capped at 31 base iterations plus nonnegative configured keyword and normal refresh budgets; sale scanning at 64 iterations; ordinary fusion and system fusion at 31 each. On exhaustion, each path logs and raises the existing `Shop.RestartGame`, allowing `in_shop()` to stop the current shop task through its established restart handling. Existing shop-exit (31) and enhancement-entry (11) caps remain. These bounds constrain loop iterations, not time spent in a native capture or input call.
- **Source identity:** base `main` and `origin/main` remain `d6c481dfc370f4388221c0fd3651fd05195a4221`. ## 2026-11-14 Fusion-entry Retry Bound

- **Source finding:** `Shop.enter_fuse()` had a 15-step no-match counter, but clicking `mirror/shop/fuse_to_select_keyword_assets.png` continued before decrementing it. Repeated recognition without a UI transition could therefore bypass the only bound. This was found by source review; no log or live run establishes that it occurred in practice.
- **Change:** the method now caps the whole capture/action loop at 16 iterations and retains its existing no-match model degradation. Exhaustion returns `False`. `fuse_system_gifts()` now stops when `enter_fuse()` fails, and `fuse_gift()` propagates that result instead of continuing with gift detection.
- **Regression and red evidence:** `test_enter_fuse_bounds_repeated_keyword_navigation` was run against base `d6c481df` with only the test overlay and failed when the 17th screenshot wait was reached after repeated recognized navigation clicks. The fixed test confirms 16 waits/clicks and a `False` result. `test_fuse_gift_stops_when_system_fusion_entry_fails` verifies no later gift detection occurs after entry failure.
- **Focused validation:** `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py -q -ra -p no:cacheprovider` passed **25 tests**.
- **Full snapshot:** `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_enter_fuse_full01\source` came from the `d6c481df` Git archive, SHA-256 `8AA405532B6C8C221FC801773B78E5AE67B4F3B20C91E6E4C4B7A7AAABB987CE`, and had no `.git` or `config.yaml` at creation. It included the same seven hash-verified overlays listed in the preceding Theme-pack section plus `tasks/mirror/in_shop.py` (`206EEC4812FBF9DF60AE6AC76E450CB07728DB2CCA9910CF2169B9296A79AB1B`) and `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py` (`ACF645854A11BFFF94C3C6BD7B312CD9F99B152646D8369D28D7A78B02BA0280`).
- **Full validation:** `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q -ra -p no:cacheprovider --tb=short --basetemp E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_enter_fuse_full01\pytest-temp-full --junitxml E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_enter_fuse_full01\pytest-results.xml`, run from the snapshot's `source` directory, passed **382 tests, 2 skipped in 47.29s**. ## 2026-11-14 Task-engine Start-state Recovery

- **Finding:** `TaskEngine.run()` called `back_init_menu()` when the initial detected page was outside `task.allowed_start_states`, then detected the page again and proceeded without checking that second state. A recovery failure or recognition miss could therefore start task actions on `UNKNOWN` or another disallowed page. This is a current source finding; no live incident is claimed.
- **Change:** the engine now fails closed if the post-recovery state is still disallowed: it marks the task `FAILED`, assigns `task.error`, logs and raises before `task.run()`. It checks the newly detected state rather than trusting the boolean returned by `back_init_menu()`. If that call returns `False` but the new state is allowed, the task can proceed.
- **Red and focused tests:** before the fix, the new regression failed with `DID NOT RAISE` on two consecutive `UNKNOWN` detections. The final `tests/unit/tasks/base/test_task_engine_stop.py` SHA-256 is `FC116AE1164CA668537DE0375C48F4321C89EAD9D7470F2930F450A800A4E52C`; its seven tests passed in a fresh config-free snapshot. They cover failed recovery and a `False` navigation return followed by an allowed `MAIN_MENU` detection.
- **Full-suite validation:** the final 11-overlay source snapshot passed **384 tests, 2 skipped in 46.40s**. Command: `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q -ra -p no:cacheprovider --tb=short --basetemp E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_task_state_full03\pytest-temp --junitxml E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_d6c481df_2026_11_14_task_state_full03\pytest-results.xml`, run from that snapshot's `source` directory through the authorized execution path. Both skips require Windows symbolic-link creation (`test_manifest_paths.py:238`, `test_updater_bounds.py:1656`). Full-repository Ruff, `.venv\Scripts\ruff.exe check --no-cache .`, passed on that snapshot. This final full suite includes the test-only change to a `False` navigation return followed by a valid re-detection; no production file changed after the package build.
- **Sandbox attempt:** an earlier fresh snapshot emitted errors in filesystem-mutating tests and JUnit recorded 62 errors across config persistence, resource sync, update and file-hash modules; pytest then failed to enumerate its unique basetemp with `WinError 5`. That run is excluded. The authorized rerun used a second fresh snapshot and basetemp and passed. No ACL changes or cleanup of the denied path were attempted.
- **Build source:** a disposable build snapshot from base `d6c481df` plus 11 overlays contained the current `tasks/base/task_engine.py` SHA-256 `D68D2EFD1B6CAC1387687CB94BE8A5995F3935CA716377E99E8FD946C731CD17`. This production source hash still matches the worktree; only the test file was refined after build. The build archive omitted `.Jules` and `.jules` because their tracked names collide on Windows. ## 2026-11-14 Mirror Prior-run Reward Loop Bound

- **Finding:** `Mirror.get_reward_in_road()` used an unbounded `while True`. Its 20-attempt `main_loop_count` was decremented only after no recognized action; repeated successful recognition of `claim_rewards_confirm_assets.png` or another action branch continued without consuming that budget. A recognized click that failed to advance the screen could therefore keep the prior-run reward flow running indefinitely. This is a source-level control-flow finding; no live incident is claimed.
- **Change:** the loop now allows at most 31 capture/action iterations and raises the existing `cannotOperateGameError` when that total bound is exhausted. The 20-attempt no-match counter and model degradation remain unchanged, and the existing success path still returns early.

The fixed path verifies 31 captures and 31 recognized confirmation clicks before the expected terminal error.

## 2026-11-14 Historical Reward-card Iteration Bound Checkpoint

- **Source finding:** `get_reward_card()` decremented its no-action retry counter only after the state-specific branches. Recognizing and clicking the "continue choosing" control, or retrying a still-visible claim button after the delay, executed `continue` before that counter changed. A nonadvancing screen could therefore repeat successful action detections indefinitely. The only caller in `Mirror.run()` ignores the function's boolean return, so returning `False` after internal exhaustion would permit the outer state loop to invoke it again. This is a source-level finding; no live incident is claimed.
- **Implementation:** `get_reward_card()` now caps total capture/action iterations at 31 and raises `cannotOperateGameError` on exhaustion. It preserves the `False` return when `retry()` reports that the game restarted, allowing the outer loop to take a fresh capture. The count still controls the existing recognition-model degradation on no-action attempts.
- **Regression coverage:** `test_reward_card_selection_bounds_repeated_continue_clicks` verifies exactly 31 clicks before the terminal error. `test_reward_card_selection_accepts_success_on_last_iteration` verifies an expected page transition on iteration 31 still succeeds. Both are in `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py`.
- **Overlay source identity:** base HEAD `d6c481dfc370f4388221c0fd3651fd05195a4221`; `git archive HEAD` ZIP SHA-256 `69362C57BE4ED76E355DE62E3199DA74D93035486F14397C46C29E1E029FF741`. ## 2026-11-14 Bounded Theme-pack Retry and Current Package

- **Source finding:** `select_theme_pack()` had two retry paths that skipped the existing `loop_count`: a recognized difficulty-button click continued immediately, and a caught recognition/selection exception logged then continued. Repeated clicks or a persistent exception could therefore prevent the normal retry budget from reaching fallback.
- **Implementation:** the selector now caps the whole capture/action flow at 32 iterations, matching the existing 32-screenshot allowance in its no-text difficulty path. Exhaustion reaches the existing return-to-menu fallback. Existing choice and recognition-model behavior is otherwise unchanged.
- **Regression coverage:** new tests reproduce repeated difficulty-button clicks and repeated exceptions, asserting 32 screenshot attempts followed by one fallback. ## 2026-11-14 Bounded Shop Exit and Enhancement Entry Actions

- **Source and finding:** `Shop.in_shop()` had a 31-iteration no-match recovery counter, but the leave-confirm, leave-shop, and heal-return click branches all used `continue` before decrementing it. The enhancement-list entry phase in `Shop.enhance_gifts()` likewise continued after a recognized entry click without consuming its 10-step no-match counter. In either path a recognized action without a page transition could repeat indefinitely. These are source-level control-flow findings, not reported live incidents.
- **Change:** the shop-exit phase now allows at most 31 total screenshot/action iterations. Enhancement-list entry allows at most 11 total attempts. Existing no-match model escalation, `back_init_menu()` fallback, and enhancement failure/restart behavior remain.
- **Regression:** `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py::test_shop_exit_caps_repeated_confirm_clicks` verifies 31 repeated confirm clicks and one fallback. `test_enhance_gifts_caps_repeated_entry_clicks` verifies 11 repeated entry clicks, then the existing failure return without entering the enhancement loop. ## 2026-11-14 Bounded Mirror Restart Actions

- **Source finding:** `Mirror.re_start()` continued after recognized forfeit, give-up, or settings clicks without advancing a total retry budget. Since its callers do not reliably stop on a `False` return, a repeated recognized click without a page transition could leave the restart flow issuing input indefinitely. This is a source-level finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** restart now allows at most 31 capture/action iterations. A confirmation recognized on iteration 31 still succeeds; if the screen does not advance, exhaustion raises `cannotOperateGameError` so the task fails through its established exception path.
- **Regression:** `test_restart_bounds_repeated_forfeit_clicks` failed against the old source at the forbidden 32nd click. The fixed tests cover exactly 31 repeated clicks followed by a terminal error and confirmation success on the last allowed iteration. `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py` passed **29 tests in 33.29s**; full-repository Ruff passed.
- **Snapshot and full validation:** base HEAD `d6c481dfc370f4388221c0fd3651fd05195a4221`, `git archive HEAD` TAR SHA-256 `8AA405532B6C8C221FC801773B78E5AE67B4F3B20C91E6E4C4B7A7AAABB987CE`. The snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_mirror_restart_full01\source` overlaid 15 code/test paths after SHA-256 comparison with the worktree; `.git` and root `config.yaml` were absent before tests. The full suite passed **408 tests, 2 skipped in 64.81s**. Both skips require unavailable Windows symbolic-link creation (`tests/unit/module/resource_sync/test_manifest_paths.py:238`, `tests/unit/updater/test_updater_bounds.py:1656`). ## 2026-11-15 Bounded Three-floor Exit Actions

- **Source finding:** In `Mirror.run()`, when `floor_3_exit` is enabled and `self.floor >= 4`, recognized clicks on the forfeit or settings controls continued before reaching the main loop's retry accounting. A nonadvancing screen could repeat those recognized actions indefinitely. This is a source-level control-flow finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** The exit branch now allows at most 31 recognized forfeit/settings clicks. It checks the forfeit-confirm control first on every cycle, so confirmation appearing after the 31st action still succeeds. If confirmation is absent, it raises `cannotOperateGameError` before another click; the existing task worker reports this terminal failure to the user.
- **Regression coverage:** two parametrized cases hold the forfeit or settings control recognized and verify exactly 31 clicks, then terminal failure before a 32nd action. A third case verifies the confirmation succeeds on the observation after the final allowed click. ## 2026-11-15 Bounded Road-map EGO Gift Confirm Actions

- **Source finding:** both the `ROAD_MAP` branch and the fallback confirmation path in `Mirror.run()` clicked `ego_gift_get_confirm_assets.png` and immediately continued. These recognized actions skipped `main_loop_count` accounting; a stalled confirmation reproduced an attempted 32nd click. This is a source/control-flow finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** both call sites now use one counter with a 31-click limit. After each later screenshot, `find_element` checks whether the same confirmation remains visible, including while other state handlers run. Disappearance resets the counter; continued visibility after click 31 raises `cannotOperateGameError` before another click. The limit therefore applies to a repeated visible confirmation within one `run()` invocation rather than accumulating across separate confirmations.

## 2026-11-15 Bounded Infinity Mirror Popup Handling

Disappearance resets the counter; continued visibility after iteration 31 raises `cannotOperateGameError` before another iteration or close attempt.

## 2026-11-15 Bounded Road-map No-marker Retries

- **Source finding:** the outer page dispatcher can recognize `GameState.ROAD_MAP`, after which the branch captures again before searching for `mirror/road_in_mir/legend_assets.png` or `mirror/road_in_mir/to_window_assets.png`. When both markers disappeared on those later captures, the branch continued without consuming the general loop retry count. Against the prior source, `test_mirror_run_bounds_road_map_when_route_markers_disappear` reproduced more than 96 screenshot calls. This is a source-level control-flow finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** `tasks/mirror/mirror.py` adds `_MAX_ROAD_MAP_NO_MARKER_ITERATIONS = 31` and a `Mirror.run()` counter. Each no-marker route-map observation increments it; once 31 misses have occurred, the next such observation raises `cannotOperateGameError` before another route-map cycle. Finding either marker resets the count and preserves the existing `search_road()` behavior. The count also resets on restart recovery, `retry() is False`, non-road-map state dispatch, recognized overlay handling in the road-map branch, and the configured three-floor exit path.
- **Regression coverage:** `test_mirror_run_bounds_road_map_when_route_markers_disappear` confirms terminal failure after 96 screenshots (32 route-map cycles, three captures each; the final cycle is the terminal observation after 31 misses). `test_mirror_run_recovers_road_map_marker_after_retry_limit` confirms that the legend marker returning on screenshot 96 invokes `search_road()` and dispatches onward. The focused file passed **40 tests in 33.05s**.

## 2026-11-15 Bounded Three-floor Exit Without Recognized Controls

- **Source finding:** the existing `floor_3_exit` branch already caps recognized forfeit/settings clicks at 31 and checks the confirmation action first. However, when both controls were unrecognized and the page dispatcher returned `ROAD_MAP`, that branch continued without reaching the general loop counter. The unrecognized-control path could therefore retry indefinitely. This is a source-level control-flow finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** `Mirror.run()` now counts route-map iterations while `floor_3_exit` is active at `self.floor >= 4` and raises `cannotOperateGameError` after 31 consecutive iterations without a recognized forfeit/settings control. The existing confirmation check remains ahead of this bound and the recognized-action bound; page changes, restart recovery, `retry() is False`, or a recognized exit action reset the no-action count. This does not assert that any visual control or transition was recognized correctly in a live game.
- **Regression coverage:** `test_mirror_run_bounds_floor_three_exit_when_controls_are_not_recognized` reproduces exhaustion and asserts the surfaced error after 31 no-action route-map cycles. `test_mirror_run_accepts_floor_three_exit_confirmation_after_no_action_limit` verifies the confirmation is accepted on the next observation after those 31 cycles. ## 2026-11-15 Bounded Empty-sinner Reformation Recovery

- **Source finding:** in `Mirror.run()`, `BATTLE_FORMATION` may click `teams/none_sinner_assets.png`, set `first_battle = True`, and `continue`; the next cycle calls `team_formation()` and repeats if the marker remains. `team_formation()` returns after issuing the configured clicks and does not verify the page transition. Separately, the localized `battle/select_none_assets.png` resource says there are unselected Sinners and asks to deploy all 12; its fallback dismisses with a blank click, sets `first_battle = True`, and continues. Both paths bypass the general loop counter. This is a source/control-flow finding, not a reported live incident.
- **Implementation:** `Mirror.run()` shares `_MAX_EMPTY_SINNER_RECOVERIES = 3` across both recovery paths. The empty-slot path raises `cannotOperateGameError` if the marker remains after three successful recovery triggers, before another click; the popup fallback raises before a fourth dismissal attempt. `UNKNOWN` detections preserve the counter because they do not prove that the page changed. `check_and_recover_process()` recovery, `retry() is False`, the explicit team-check restart path, a recognized different page, or disappearance of the empty-slot marker resets it.
- **Regression evidence:** an initial test-only reproduction against the prior implementation reached a fourth `teams/none_sinner_assets.png` click and failed before the guard. Final regressions cover repeated empty-slot detection with intermittent `UNKNOWN`, repeated unselected-sinner popup detection while the dispatcher remains `UNKNOWN`, and accepting a recognized completion after the last allowed reformation. The focused `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py` passed **45 tests in 40.17s**.

The filtered archive SHA-256 was `4A3FF6A45ED59A6F9283FEA18490F04A47F09A5A1626A0D8EAEBF5801EDF31D3`. The four changed code/test overlays were copied from the worktree and SHA-256 matched:

- **Finding and fix:** `tasks/base/retry.py::retry()` returns `False` when its freeze-recovery path restarts the game and otherwise returns `None`. `Mirror.check_and_recover_process()` treated that ordinary `None` result as a retry failure and redundantly killed/restarted the game. Other Mirror fallback, reward, prior-run reward, and event paths, plus the mail-claim path, could keep acting on state captured before a restart or report success after restart. The affected paths now propagate `False` or return to the outer state loop so the next cycle takes a fresh screenshot. The remaining bare calls in `Mirror.run()` immediately continue to the next outer iteration, which waits for a fresh screenshot before dispatch.
- **Regression coverage:** added tests for network recovery without duplicate restart, fresh-state handling in road-map and unknown fallbacks, both event batch-click branches, reward and prior-run reward propagation, and stopping mail claim before closing a stale screen.

Full-repository `ruff check --no-cache .` and `compileall -q app module tasks utils scripts main.py main_dev.py updater.py` passed.

## 2026-11-15 PR #142 Review Gate (Superseded by Post-Merge Verification)

- **Review baseline and remote state:** at review time PR #142 (`https://github.com/AscAed/AhabAssistantLimbusCompany/pull/142`) was open with code head `923bd3160fbebd92f3d16f3891c1b4b2992cc335` and base `main` at `2cefeb6a3a526ddd85a65fda3b541cddaeaf526a`. GitHub reported the branch mergeable. Its visible CodeRabbit check succeeded, while the review still had one actionable inline finding; the green check does not mean the PR is finding-free.
- **Finding:** `tasks/daily/get_prize.py::_has_meaningful_screen_change()` treats at least 0.5% changed grayscale pixels across two frames as a loaded page. A sustained loading overlay can satisfy that rule while the previous tab remains visible. The function can then report success for an empty daily/weekly result before the target content appears.
- **Evidence inspected:** the current source and tests; `assets/images/default/share/pass/pass_coin.png`; localized `pass_missions_assets.png` and `weekly_assets.png` navigation templates; PR review comments and check state. The pass templates are labels rather than selected/loaded-page markers. No full-frame pass-page fixture is present under `tests/`; current tests use synthetic PIL frames and do not exercise a real page or loading overlay.
- **Disposition:** the finding is valid. No source fix is recorded because the current checkout has no verified page-specific no-claim signal; inventing one risks either accepting the wrong page or turning a normal no-reward result into a task failure. Keep PR #142 open and do not merge into `main`. To continue, establish a page-specific signal from an authorized, sanitized sample with provenance/privacy/use-rights recorded, or decide how the task should report an ambiguous no-claim state.

## 2026-11-15 CodeRabbit Review Fixes and Final Local Validation

- **Review source and findings:** CodeRabbit reviewed the committed pass-list change at `1e9afb6b2a90b313a6778b1264cfe1fc7d0c1305` and identified three valid workflow defects. When `Mirror.run()` had completed but `back_init_menu()` raised, completion accounting was lost. The loading-timeout branch of `check_times()` could still kill/restart the game when `allow_restart=False`. EXP/thread navigation could detect the team screen on the final bounded iteration but fail to return success.
- **Fixes and regressions:** `onetime_mir_process()` now maps menu-recovery exceptions after a completed Mirror run to `COMPLETED_RECOVERY_FAILED`, while preserving `userStopError`. `check_times()` and `retry()` now carry `allow_restart`; disabled-restart timeouts return failure without killing or restarting the game. EXP/thread navigation now returns success only on the team-screen marker, including on the last retry; the tutorial prompt no longer counts as confirmed team selection. Focused review regressions passed **7 tests** after reproducing the defects against the pre-fix source.

## 2026-11-15 Second CodeRabbit Finding: Mirror Post-run Resource Preparation

- **Finding:** CodeRabbit's follow-up review of commit `7d21b2ea` found that, after `Mirror.run()` and menu recovery both succeeded, an exception from `make_enkephalin_module()` escaped to the general handler and returned `False`. The batch then treated the completed dungeon as a failed attempt and retried it, leaving completed-run and hard-mode counts unchanged.
- **Fix and regression:** `onetime_mir_process()` now returns `COMPLETED_POSTPROCESS_FAILED` for non-stop resource-preparation exceptions after a successful dungeon. `Mirror_task()` records the completed run and decrements hard-mode chance, then stops the batch and returns failure. `userStopError` still propagates. The new integration regression first failed on the old behavior with three Mirror launches; after the fix, the task workflow test file passed **46 tests** and asserted one launch, one completion update, and one hard-mode decrement.

A missing payload fails during extraction before process termination or app launch. Therefore a real update archive is not suitable for an unconstrained smoke check.

- **Source finding:** the `shopping_strategy` UI map in `app/__init__.py` labels index `4` “不购买回血饰品” (do not buy healing gifts). The corresponding conditions in `Shop.buy_gifts()` still permit Level III/IV priority purchases and system-gift purchases. Before this fix, one `complete_count` accumulated purchases across must-buy, Level III/IV and system-gift paths, then `sort_points()` used that total to truncate primary- and second-system template matches. This allowed a completed purchase from one category to suppress an unrelated candidate in another template list.

## 2026-11-17 Bounded Mirror Network Recovery

- **Source review:** in the current dirty checkout at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`, `Mirror.run()` calls `check_and_recover_process()` again after a network recovery returns. `tasks.base.retry.retry()` can return `False` after timeout, while the caller still returns to the outer state loop; a persistent network marker could therefore trigger repeated recovery cycles without a Mirror-level total bound.
- **Stage plan and result:** (1) audit the checker/caller and add bounded-recovery regressions, complete; both tests failed against the implementation without the bound. (2) allow three consecutive recovery calls, stop with `cannotOperateGameError` on the next marker, and reset after the marker clears or process recovery begins, complete. (3) run focused and full offline validation, inspect the resulting diff, and update this handoff, complete. This is a local source reliability fix and does not change retry internals or live-game behavior claims.
- **Implementation:** `Mirror` now tracks network recovery attempts per instance. The first three marker detections call the existing `retry()` helper. A fourth consecutive detection fails with `cannotOperateGameError`; a check with no connection marker resets the counter. The process-not-running branch resets it before restart recovery. Existing retry/restart behavior is unchanged within those bounds.
- **Regression evidence:** tests cover the three-call limit and fourth-call failure, counter reset after markers clear, unchanged retry ownership (no duplicate game restart), and reset on successful process recovery. The pre-fix snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_network_recovery_20261117_02\source` produced the intended two assertion failures: no exception at the fourth call and no counter reset after markers cleared.
- **Current-source validation:** config-free-at-creation snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_network_recovery_20261117_04\source` was copied from the worktree excluding root `config.yaml*`, `config_backup`, `.git`, `.venv`, logs, build outputs, caches, `.Jules`/`.jules`, `.tmp_pytest`, and `test-run-temp`. Both modified source/test files matched the worktree by SHA-256 at snapshot creation: `tasks/mirror/mirror.py` `19DC2037F40B22D9C85212D30625F7DF74C79328C3BCE08DC5177B007F9DBFD6`; `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py` `E3EF89F04A16ED94C54DD7AB13117BE9AAAAF12F7ACA463C0F2D51FE41C45F25`. The focused file passed **68 tests in 37.32s**. The full suite passed **541 tests, 2 skipped of 543 collected in 71.86s**; both skips require unavailable Windows symbolic-link creation. Changed-file Ruff and `python -B -m compileall -q tasks/mirror/mirror.py` passed. Tests ran in a disposable snapshot; any generated config remained there and was not read.
- **Current boundary and next step:** no app, updater, game, or package was launched or built in this source-only follow-up. The existing app-runtime gate still requires a fresh process-absence check and explicit authorization of the exact executable hash. The next source evidence item remains manually labeled incomplete, claimable, and already-claimed pass screens before changing pass reward behavior; the required examples and task completion policy remain open. ## 2026-11-17 Mirror Team-selection Failure Propagation

- **Source finding:** `select_mirror_team()` returned `None` after its bounded entry-state recognition was exhausted and it attempted menu recovery. It already returned `False` when `retry()` failed. Both the `MIRROR_TEAM_SELECT` branch and the fallback team-selection branch in `Mirror.run()` ignored that failure and dispatched another state cycle. `onetime_mir_process()` treats a false `Mirror.run()` result as a failed run, so returning `False` stops this attempt through the existing task-result path.
- **Stage plan and result:** (1) trace terminal returns and both callers; complete. (2) add regressions for recognition exhaustion and both caller paths; complete; the three cases failed before the fix by proving implicit `None` or a further state dispatch. (3) make success/failure explicit, propagate failure, validate, and update handoff; complete.
- **Implementation:** `select_mirror_team()` now has a `bool` result contract: `True` after entry recognition, `False` after retry failure or exhausted recognition/menu recovery. Both `Mirror.run()` call sites immediately return `False` if selection fails. Team lookup errors and bounded screenshot failures keep their existing exceptions.

## 2026-11-17 Mirror Shop Failure Propagation

- **Checkout and stage plan:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`; the worktree had existing user/session edits and no staged paths. Stage 3 (propagate explicit `False` at each boundary): complete. Stage 4 (focused/full tests, lint, compile and handoff): complete.
- **Dependency, risk, and acceptance:** this change depends on existing explicit `False` contracts and adds no package or fixture dependency. Accept when a failed fusion or enhancement returns from `Shop.in_shop()` before its exit scan, the Mirror wrapper preserves `False`, and `Mirror.run()` returns after one SHOP dispatch; existing implicit `None` success remains unchanged. The mocks do not qualify OCR recognition or real shop behavior.
- **Finding and implementation:** `Shop.fuse_gift()` returns explicit `False` when fusion-screen entry fails, and `Shop.enhance_gifts()` returns `False` when the enhancement list cannot be opened after its bounded attempts. The shop loop previously ignored both results and proceeded toward exit. It now returns `False` immediately for either result; `Mirror.in_shop()` preserves the shop return; `Mirror.run()` records elapsed time and stops on `False`. Normal implicit `None` success behavior and `RestartGame` exception handling are unchanged. This batch covers these two explicit `False` results; other shop helpers and their behavior were not modified.
- **Regression evidence:** `test_shop_stops_when_fusion_returns_failure`, `test_shop_stops_when_enhancement_returns_failure`, and `test_mirror_run_stops_when_shop_handler_fails` failed against pre-fix source by returning `None` or continuing dispatch. `test_mirror_in_shop_returns_shop_failure` checks wrapper propagation.

Root `config.yaml`, `config_backup`, `dist`, `.git`, and `.venv` were absent before test execution; after validation, `config.yaml` and `config_backup` were present inside the disposable snapshot. - **Plan and finding:** Stage 1 (trace `retry()` timeout bookkeeping and `ensure_simulator_game_started()`): complete. ## 2026-11-18 Grandet Lunacy-spend Guard and Current-source Package

- **Checkout, scope, and staged plan:** `HEAD` and `origin/main` remain `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`; the worktree was already dirty and unstaged. Stage 1 (audit the Grandet timing result contract across 26/52/78 spending options and the task error path): complete. Stage 2 (reproduce unsafe/mistimed confirmations, define OCR tri-state results, and add fail-closed handling): complete. Stage 3 (focused, related, full-suite, and static validation): complete. Stage 4 (isolated package build and archive verification): complete. Stage 5 (runtime launch/exit qualification): not started. This batch changed only `tasks/base/make_enkephalin_module.py`, `tests/unit/tasks/base/test_enkephalin_stop.py`, `tests/unit/tasks/base/test_make_enkephalin_ocr.py`, `docs/PROJECT.md`, `docs/development/implementation-roadmap.md`, and this handoff. No files were staged or committed.
- **Source finding:** `get_the_timing()` previously returned `None` for a missing lunacy-panel anchor, `True` for a recognized timer satisfying the existing `minute >= 5 and seconds >= 20` predicate, and `False` both for a timer that did not satisfy that predicate and for missing/invalid OCR. Spend confirmation previously treated every result except `False` as ready, so a missing anchor could reach the confirmation click. The 52/78 options also set `Grandet = True` after one unsuccessful 2-second poll; the 26 option waited for a ready result. `Buy_enkephalin()` does not swallow exceptions from `lunacy_to_enkephalin()`, and `TaskEngine` marks thrown task errors failed before attempting local recovery.
- **Red regression evidence:** six parameterized spend regressions cover all 26/52/78 options: three verify that `[False, False, True]` timing results are all consumed before confirmation; three verify a missing anchor prevents confirmation and raises `EnkephalinPreparationError`. Against the old spend flow, the red run reported **5 failed, 1 passed, 7 deselected**. Six additional timing-contract cases cover absent OCR, empty OCR, malformed text, a valid early timer, a ready timer, and `return_time`; against the prior parser, the first three failed (**3 failed, 3 passed, 8 deselected**). These are offline mock reproductions, not real-game observations.
- **Implementation:** `get_the_timing()` now returns `True` only when valid timer OCR satisfies the existing `minute >= 5 and seconds >= 20` predicate, `False` for a valid timer that does not satisfy it, and `None` for a missing anchor or unavailable/unparseable OCR. For `return_time=True`, valid input still returns seconds and unavailable/invalid input returns `None`. `_wait_for_grandet_timing(already_qualified)` continues stop-aware 2-second polling on valid `False`, marks qualification only after explicit `True`, preserves later-spend behavior after qualification, and raises `EnkephalinPreparationError` on `None` before confirmation. `ProductionWork` already treats `None` as an unavailable timer and skips the cycle after its existing bounded 60-read attempt; no caller configuration or click amount changed. Repeated valid-but-not-ready `False` readings have no independent wall-clock deadline, but remain cooperatively stoppable.
- **Exact-source validation:** config-free-at-creation snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_grandet_ocr_contract_20261118_02\source` was based on the prior validated snapshot and overlaid with the current production and test files; the personal root config and `config_backup` were excluded. ## 2026-11-18 Mirror Entry and Event Result Contract

- **Workspace and scope:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty and remains unstaged; this batch changed `tasks/mirror/mirror.py`, `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py`, `docs/PROJECT.md`, `docs/development/implementation-roadmap.md`, and this handoff. - **Finding:** `road_to_mir()` had recognized success paths and bounded exhaustion paths that fell through to implicit `None`; `Mirror.run()` stops only on explicit `False`, so a failed entry could continue. `event_handling()` likewise returned implicit `None` after both successful transition and bounded no-transition paths, allowing callers to misclassify the result.
- **Red regressions:** three focused cases failed against the old implementation: repeated successful drive clicks exhausted `road_to_mir()` without `False`, and the event handler returned `None` for both a capped no-transition path and a recognized transition. These failures were reproduced in disposable snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_mirror_entry_result_contract_20261118_01\source` as **3 failed, 83 deselected**.
- **Implementation:** `road_to_mir()` now returns `True` for recognized existing-shop, route-map, resume, Mirror-entry, team-star, star-coin and theme-pack paths; it returns `False` after bounded loop exhaustion or failed menu recovery. `event_handling()` tracks whether a transition was recognized and returns `True` only for that case, otherwise `False` after its bounded loop or recovery path. Existing input, retry, and `RestartGame` behavior was otherwise left intact.
- **Exact-source validation:** final config-free-at-creation snapshot `E:\PUBLIC\Project\AALC\.codex_validation_mirror_entry_result_contract_20261118_03\source` matched **15** production/config-example inputs by SHA-256. `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_mirror_capture_recovery.py` passed **86 tests**; `tests/unit/tasks/mirror` passed **163 tests**; the complete suite command passed **589 tests, 2 skipped of 591 collected in 102.06s**. ## 2026-11-18 Mirror Shop Exit-Failure Propagation

- **Workspace and scope:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty and remains unstaged. This batch changed `tasks/mirror/in_shop.py`, strengthened `tests/unit/tasks/mirror/test_in_shop_basics.py`, and updated this handoff, `docs/PROJECT.md`, and `docs/development/implementation-roadmap.md`. Existing user/session changes were preserved; no commit was created. The inaccessible `.tmp_pytest/` and `test-run-temp/` paths were not touched.
- **Finding:** after the bounded shop-exit loop exhausted, the counter-exhaustion branch and the loop `else` branch both called `back_init_menu()` but then returned implicit `None`. Because `Mirror.run()` stops the SHOP branch only on explicit `False`, a shop that failed to leave could continue the outer Mirror workflow.

## 2026-11-18 Lunacy-to-Enkephalin Purchase-count Propagation

- **Workspace and scope:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty and remains unstaged. This batch changed `tasks/base/make_enkephalin_module.py`, added a parameterized regression to `tests/unit/tasks/base/test_enkephalin_stop.py`, and updated this handoff, `docs/PROJECT.md`, and `docs/development/implementation-roadmap.md`. Existing user/session changes were preserved; no commit was created. The inaccessible `.tmp_pytest/` and `test-run-temp/` paths were not touched.
- **Finding:** `assets/config/config.example.yaml:117` defines `set_lunacy_to_enkephalin` as 0/1/2/3 purchases, and `app/__init__.py:44-48` exposes the corresponding no/first/second/third conversion choices. `lunacy_to_enkephalin()` previously kept the original `times` value after each confirmed 26/52/78-lunacy choice. A persistent spend marker could therefore cause repeated confirmations beyond the configured purchase count. This is a control-flow/config-contract finding, not a live overpurchase report.
- **Red regression and implementation:** `test_lunacy_spending_respects_configured_purchase_count` covers five cases: one, two, or three repeated 26-lunacy confirmations, a 52-lunacy bundled confirmation, and a 78-lunacy bundled confirmation. Against the old source each case exceeded its configured confirmation budget and failed with `spending exceeded configured purchase count`. The function now copies `times` into `remaining_times` and subtracts 1, 2, or 3 after the corresponding confirmation click. Grandet timing qualification, screenshot cadence, click targets, panel close, and existing stop/error behavior are unchanged.

## 2026-11-18 Mirror Incompatible-team Queue Bound

- **Workspace and scope:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty and remains unstaged. - **Finding and red regression:** `Mirror_task()` could keep rotating an active queue indefinitely when every active team was incompatible with the selected difficulty while legacy or malformed state bypassed normal configuration normalization. - **Workspace and scope:** local `main` and `origin/main` remain at `cc9e3724876b81be7bbb201c1bb25d2d008c5c57`. The worktree was already dirty and remains unstaged.
